Gold — Financial Astrology Model v1.0 (Brihat Samhita Ch.41), 20 Nov 2026 – 31 Dec 2027

Classical Combined Commodity Framework: Brihat Samhita 41.2/41.7 (gold = Mesha and Makara) and 41.9–13 (planets counted from those signs), with BPHS Shadbala for "strong", Sarvashtakavarga and Parashari aspects. Daily chart: New York at Hindu sunrise, sidereal Lahiri. Two separate indices: scarcity / price pressure (S) and demand / market value (V), −100 to +100, gold = average of the Aries and Capricorn readings. Weights (placement 45%, strength 25%, aspects 20%, Ashtakavarga 10%) and cut-offs are documented research conventions, not classical prescriptions. Silver: Insufficient data — Ch.41 assigns no sign to silver. Method: Reference_materials/extracted/FINANCIAL_ASTROLOGY_MODEL_v1.md.
